### [1] [An Empirical Study of Handcrafted Feature Learning and Convolutional Neural Networks for Facial Expression Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15288)
*Chethiya Galkaduwa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在FER-2013、CK+和KDEF三个数据集上系统比较了HOG+SVM、LBP+逻辑回归与轻量级CNN在面部表情识别中的表现。CNN在复杂数据上表现最佳，HOG在受控环境下表现良好，LBP在所有数据集上均表现不佳。研究表明数据集复杂度显著影响模型性能，鲁棒的特征学习对真实场景的面部表情识别至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管CNN在现代面部表情识别中占主导地位，但传统手工特征描述符（如HOG、LBP）仍作为经典基线被使用。然而，现有研究缺乏对这些方法在多个代表性数据集上的系统对比，特别是考虑到数据集复杂度（从受控环境到自然场景）对性能的影响。

Method: 采用三种方法进行对比：(1) HOG特征提取结合SVM分类器；(2) LBP特征提取结合逻辑回归分类器；(3) 轻量级CNN。在三个具有不同复杂度的数据集（FER-2013、CK+、KDEF）上进行实验评估。

Result: CNN在总体性能上最优，尤其在复杂数据（FER-2013）上优势明显；HOG在受控环境（CK+）下表现强劲；LBP在所有数据集上均表现最差。数据集复杂度是影响各方法性能差异的关键因素。

Conclusion: CNN的端到端特征学习能力使其更适合复杂、非受控的真实场景；HOG在受控环境下仍具实用价值；LBP的局限性限制了其在现代FER任务中的应用。未来的研究应关注如何在保证鲁棒性的同时降低模型复杂度。

Abstract: Facial expression recognition is an important computer vision task with applications in human--computer interaction, mental health monitoring, driver alert systems, and behavioral analysis. While convolutional neural networks (CNNs) dominate modern facial expression recognition, handcrafted feature descriptors such as Histogram of Oriented Gradients (HOG) and Local Binary Patterns (LBP) remain useful classical baselines. This study compares HOG with Support Vector Machine (SVM), LBP with Logistic Regression, and a lightweight CNN across three facial expression datasets: FER-2013, CK+, and KDEF. The results show that CNNs achieve the best overall performance, particularly on more complex data, while HOG performs strongly in controlled environments. LBP performs poorly across all datasets. The study highlights that dataset complexity significantly affects performance and that robust feature learning is essential for real-world facial expression recognition.

</details>


### [2] [Rethinking the Readout: Unlocking Video Backbones for AI-Generated Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15321)
*Manni Cui,Ziheng Qin,ZiAn Wang,Ruiqi Liu,Dianyuan Zou,Jianglan Wei,Han Zhou,Yu Liu,Jingrui Xu,Wenhao Wang,Zhenyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对AI生成视频中时序伪影检测问题，提出Velocity Gated Patch Velocity Profiling (V-PVP)方法，通过替换视频骨干网络的全局聚合层为双流补丁速度场处理模块（仅增加0.5M参数），在冻结骨干网络条件下达到95.28 AUC，显著提升跨骨干网络的AIGV检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频骨干网络在AIGV检测中未能超越强图像预训练模型，归因于其全局读取方式将每帧压缩为单一全局描述符，抑制了局部补丁级时序动态和补丁间关系，而这正是AIGV检测最依赖的线索。

Method: 提出V-PVP轻量级读取模块，替换原始聚合层为两个并行流（速度门控+补丁速度分析）处理补丁速度场，仅增加约0.5M可训练参数，可作为即插即用模块适配多种视频骨干网络，支持端到端微调和线性探测两种设置。

Result: 在AIGVDBench上达到95.28 AUC，且骨干网络完全冻结。V-PVP在多种视频骨干网络上一致提升性能，恢复了冻结视频骨干网络在AIGV检测上的时序潜力优势。

Conclusion: 仅替换聚合层即可重新激活冻结视频骨干网络的时序潜力，恢复其对AIGV检测的优势，表明现有视频骨干网络的时序能力被全局读取方式所抑制而非缺失。

Abstract: AI-generated videos (AIGVs) typically contain subtle temporal artifacts that arise from inter-frame inconsistencies rather than within individual frames. A detector that captures such artifacts should therefore benefit from video pretrained backbones over image only ones. In practice, however, video backbones with standard global readouts often fail to outperform strong image pretrained probes on AIGV benchmarks. We attribute this gap to excessive spatiotemporal aggregation in the readout. Video pretrained backbones tend to compress each frame into a single global descriptor. This compression suppresses local patch level temporal dynamics and discards inter patch relations, which are precisely the cues that AIGV detection most reliably depends on. Based on this, we propose Velocity Gated Patch Velocity Profiling (V-PVP), a lightweight readout that replaces only the aggregation layer with two parallel streams over the patch velocity field, adding only about $0.5$M trainable parameters. V-PVP serves as a general plug-and-play module that consistently improves performance across diverse video backbones under both end-to-end fine-tuning and linear probing settings. Our method reaches \textbf{95.28} AUC on AIGVDBench while keeping the backbone fully frozen. The results show that simply replacing the aggregation layer reactivates the temporal potential of frozen video backbones, restoring their advantage on AIGV detection. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/PVP-81B3/.

</details>


### [3] [Training-Free Open-Vocabulary 3D Point-Cloud Segmentation on the Generalized Few-Shot Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15331)
*Silas kwabla Gah,Ebenezer Owusu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种完全无需训练、无需3D标签、无需few-shot支持的开放词汇3D点云分割方法。通过冻结的3D视觉-语言模型（RegionPLC）与冻结的提示概念分割器（SAM3）协同，利用跨视图一致性（仅依赖类别名称）来分割新类，在ScanNet200上提升新类mIoU +2.6，并恢复了与全监督SOTA之间33%的新类差距。在更难的ScanNet++上，新类mIoU从16.2提升至31.9。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GFS-PCS方法需要基类3D标签、逐episode训练和support标注，代价高昂。本文探索在不使用任何训练、3D标签甚至few-shot support的情况下，能否仅通过无训练的开源组件和跨视图一致性来实现令人满意的少样本新类分割。

Method: 使用冻结的RegionPLC作为稠密3D视觉-语言先验，冻结的SAM3作为可提示的概念分割器，仅用新类名称作为提示，从带位姿的RGB视图中提升（lift）2D分割到3D。关键创新是通过跨视图一致性滤波：一个3D点只有当足够多的观测视图一致认为它属于某新类时才被接受。此外还尝试了融合门和原型密集分类器注入few-shot支持，但发现这些反而会降低性能。

Result: ScanNet200上：新类mIoU比无训练稠密先验提升+2.6，基类精度损失<0.5，恢复33%的新类差距。ScanNet++上：新类mIoU从16.2提升至31.9（+15.7），基类下降1.7，调和平均从21.5提升至31.1。注入few-shot支持反而会降低性能。

Conclusion: 跨视图一致性是使无训练开放词汇3D点云分割可行的关键，而few-shot支持在该框架中并非必要甚至有害。这挑战了现有GFS-PCS方法对大量标注和训练依赖的范式，展示了通过冻结模型和几何约束即可实现有竞争力的开放词汇3D分割。

Abstract: Generalized few-shot 3D point-cloud segmentation (GFS-PCS) asks a model to segment a scene into many base classes seen at training time and a set of novel classes. The state of the art reaches novel classes by reconciling a dense but noisy 3D vision-language prior with the few-shot support, but it pays for this with base 3D labels, per-episode training, and the support annotations themselves. We ask how far the same reconciliation can go with none of these: no training, no 3D labels, and not even the few-shot support. We pair a frozen 3D vision-language model (RegionPLC) as a dense prior with a frozen promptable concept segmenter (SAM3), prompted by the bare novel class names and lifted from posed RGB views, and reconcile the two by cross-view consistency: a point becomes novel only when enough of the views that see it agree. On the ScanNet200 GFS-PCS benchmark this fully training-free, open-vocabulary pipeline improves novel mIoU by +2.6 over the training-free dense prior while holding base accuracy within 0.5, and recovers a third (33%) of the novel-class gap to the trained state of the art that uses far more supervision. We further show that injecting the few-shot support into the pipeline, as a fusion gate and as a prototypical dense classifier, adds nothing over consistency alone and in fact degrades it through the classifier, which is why the method needs no support at all. On the harder ScanNet++ benchmark, where the dense prior is far weaker on novel classes, the same pipeline nearly doubles novel mIoU (+15.7, from 16.2 to 31.9) at a 1.7 base cost, lifting the harmonic mean from 21.5 to 31.1

</details>


### [4] [Reasoning-Guided Part-Level Visual Grounding via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15374)
*Kazi Sajeed Mehrab,Hani Alomari,Najibul Haque Sarker,Chia-Wei Tang,Zaber Ibn Abdul Hakim,Anuj Karpatne,Chris Thomas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出对象-部件层次化反射式定位（OP-HRG）方法，通过粗到细的推理引导定位策略，先定位父对象再定位部件，并引入自检机制和部件感知的GRPO训练框架，使4B模型在部件定位任务上超越7B模型和SAM3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态大语言模型在根据自由形式语言查询定位整个对象时表现良好，但当查询指向对象部件而非整体时性能显著下降。作者将此归因于缺乏对象-部件的层次化结构，因为当前方法使用与定位对象相同的单步方式来定位部件。

Method: 提出OP-HRG（对象-部件层次化反射式定位），采用从粗到细的推理引导定位策略：先定位父对象，再在其内部定位部件；引入自检机制，对结果进行反思；将预测区域重新编码以检查正在修正的区域；提出部件感知的GRPO框架，使用阶段级奖励来训练整个流水线。

Result: 4B模型在PascalPart、PartImageNet和InstructPart数据集上超越了7B定位大语言模型和SAM3，并能迁移到推理分割任务。

Conclusion: 层次化推理引导的粗到细定位策略结合自检机制和部件感知的强化学习训练框架，能够有效提升MLLM在部件级定位任务上的性能，且模型参数量更小但效果更优。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) ground whole objects well from free-form language queries, but they struggle when the query names a part rather than the object. We trace this to a missing object-part hierarchy, since parts are localized in the same single step used for objects. We propose Object-Part Hierarchical Reflective Grounding (OP-HRG), a coarse-to-fine reasoning-guided grounding strategy that first localizes the parent object and then the part within it. A self-check then reflects on the result, with an extension to re-encode the predicted crop to inspect the region it is correcting. We introduce a part-aware GRPO framework to train our pipeline with stage-wise rewards. A 4B model trained this way outperforms 7B grounding LLMs and SAM3 across PascalPart, PartImageNet, and InstructPart, and transfers to reasoning segmentation.

</details>


### [5] [Partial Information Decomposition as a Multi-Contrast 3D MRI Selection Strategy for Resource-Constrained Deep Neural Network Training in Brain Tumor Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15396)
*Agamdeep Chopra,Mehmet Kurt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于部分信息分解（PID）的预训练框架，用于多对比度3D MRI分割中筛选最有效的输入组合。在T1n、T1c、T2w和T2-FLAIR四个序列中，框架选出T1c+T2-FLAIR作为最优双输入组合，其在独立测试集上的平均Dice为0.676，仅次于四输入全模态的0.687。Shapley分析同样验证T2-FLAIR和T1c是最具影响力的输入及其交互作用最强。结果表明PID预训练选择能有效识别紧凑且信息丰富的MRI输入集，降低3D模型开发的计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多对比度3D MRI分割在使用所有可用序列时计算需求高，需要一种方法在模型训练前筛选出紧凑且信息丰富的输入组合，以降低计算成本而不牺牲分割性能。

Method: 采用部分信息分解（PID）框架对输入对进行冗余、独特和协同信息排名，选择排名最高的输入对用于下游训练。训练了11个架构相同的轻量级3D U-Net，使用不同的输入配置进行比较分析，并利用Shapley值分析全输入模型中各输入及其交互作用的重要性。

Result: PID框架选出T1c+T2-FLAIR为最优双输入组合；在独立测试集上该组合平均Dice为0.676，四输入全模态为0.687，T1c+T2-FLAIR在双输入配置中最强且总体排名第二。Shapley分析确认T2-FLAIR和T1c是最具影响力的输入，其成对交互作用最强。

Conclusion: PID预训练选择框架能够在实际3D模型开发前有效地识别紧凑且信息丰富的MRI输入组合，具有显著的实用价值，可在保持较高分割性能的同时降低计算成本。

Abstract: Multi-contrast 3D MRI segmentation can be computationally demanding when all available sequences are used. We evaluate a pre-training Partial Information Decomposition framework that ranks input pairs according to their redundant, unique, and synergistic information about regional tumor burden and selects the highest-ranked pair for downstream training. Applied to T1n, T1c, T2w, and T2-FLAIR MRI, the framework selected T1c+T2-FLAIR. We then trained eleven architecturally identical lightweight 3D U-Nets using different input configurations. On an independent test cohort, T1c+T2-FLAIR was the strongest two-input configuration and ranked second overall in mean Dice (0.676 versus 0.687 for all four inputs). Independent Shapley analysis on the full-input model also identified T2-FLAIR and T1c as the most influential inputs and their pairwise interaction as the strongest. These findings demonstrate the practical value of PID based pre-training selection for identifying compact, informative MRI input sets before costly 3D model development.

</details>


### [6] [Unsupervised Keypoints for Real-Time Fall Detection: Comparative Analysis Under Real-world Conditions with Predictive Bandwidth Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15400)
*Tasmiah Haque,Jacob Kosinski,Sumit Mohan,Srinjoy Das,Mohammad Abdullah Al-Mamun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于无监督关键点和预测时序建模的隐私保护跌倒检测框架，用紧凑运动表示替代RGB视频传输。实验表明，在随机划分下无显著差异，但在受试者独立和遮挡评估下，无监督关键点对遮挡和部分可见性更鲁棒，带宽受限时优势更明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 老年人跌倒是一大安全挑战，持续监控难以维持。视频虽能捕捉跌倒姿态，但受限于隐私、计算和带宽。监督式姿态估计易受遮挡和部分身体可见性影响，亟需一种隐私保护且鲁棒的跌倒检测方案。

Method: 提出隐私保护框架：本地处理进行分割和关键点提取，用无监督关键点和变分递归预测及时序分类检测跌倒。在UR Fall Detection和Human Fall数据集上采用随机、受试者独立和基于遮挡的三种划分评估。

Result: 随机划分下两种表示无显著差异；受试者独立评估中监督关键点在解剖标志可见时更优，但无监督关键点对遮挡更鲁棒；遮挡评估中监督关键点漏检近半跌倒，无监督关键点保持高灵敏度；带宽受限时监督定位误差通过时序模型累积放大，差距进一步扩大。

Conclusion: 表示选择应基于预期视觉条件；当身体可见性受损时，无监督关键点因解剖独立性而具有优势。

Abstract: Falls among older adults are a major safety challenge, but continuous monitoring is difficult to sustain. Video captures fall-related posture and motion, yet deployment is limited by privacy, computation, and bandwidth. Supervised pose estimation is anatomically interpretable but vulnerable to occlusion and partial body visibility. We propose a privacy-preserving framework that replaces RGB transmission with compact motion representations based on unsupervised keypoints and predictive temporal modeling. Local processing performs segmentation and keypoint extraction; variational recurrent prediction and sequence classification then detect falls from observed and forecasted motion. We evaluate the framework on the UR Fall Detection and Human Fall datasets using random, subject-disjoint, and occlusion-based splits. Under random splits, neither representation consistently dominates, suggesting that standard protocols may hide meaningful differences. Under subject-disjoint evaluation, supervised keypoints show a statistically significant advantage, but performance varies by subject: they perform better when anatomical landmarks are visible, whereas unsupervised keypoints are more robust to occlusion and partial visibility, though they produce more false positives for complex activities. Under occlusion-based evaluation, supervised keypoints miss nearly half of all falls, while unsupervised keypoints retain strong sensitivity and substantially outperform them. Their anatomical independence allows spatial anchors to adapt to visible body structure rather than fail on absent landmarks. The gap widens under bandwidth constraints, where supervised localization errors compound through the temporal model. These findings show that representation choice should reflect expected visual conditions and that unsupervised keypoints offer an advantage when body visibility is compromised.

</details>


### [7] [GS-RealBlur: A Flexible Data Acquisition Framework for Real-World Image Deblurring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15401)
*Mingyang Chen,Zhilu Zhang,Honglei Xu,Renlong Wu,Xiaohe Wu,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GS-RealBlur框架，用手持相机拍摄模糊图像、用云台采集清晰图像，通过3D重建和模糊感知位姿精炼模块生成高质量配对数据，训练的去模糊模型在多个真实基准上取得最优泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像去模糊方法依赖合成数据（缺乏真实感）或复杂相机系统（缺乏灵活性），亟需一种兼具真实感与采集灵活性的真实世界模糊-清晰配对数据获取方案。

Method: 1) 手持相机拍模糊图，云台密集采集同一场景清晰图；2) 重建清晰图像的3D表示，标定模糊帧在3D中的相机位姿；3) 根据位姿渲染出清晰对应图；4) 提出Blur-aware Pose Refinement (BPR)模块，通过外观一致性和质心对齐约束精炼位姿以对齐渲染图与模糊图。

Result: 构建了高质量多样化数据集，训练的去模糊模型在多个真实世界去模糊基准上超越现有合成和真实数据集训练的模型，取得最优泛化性能。

Conclusion: GS-RealBlur实现了真实感与采集灵活性的兼顾，为真实图像去模糊提供了有效的数据获取范式，且代码和数据集将开源。

Abstract: High-quality, large-scale paired data is essential for training learning-based image deblurring models. However, synthetic blurry images generally lack realism, while real-world captured images require complex and inflexible camera systems. In this work, we propose GS-RealBlur, a data acquisition framework for real-world image deblurring, achieving both blur realism and acquisition flexibility. Specifically, we use a handheld camera to capture blurry images, and deploy a gimbal to densely capture sharp images of the same scene. We reconstruct the 3D representation of sharp images and calibrate the camera pose of each blurry frame within this 3D. The image rendered from this 3D according to the pose serves as the sharp counterpart. To better align the rendered image with the blurry image, we introduce a Blur-aware Pose Refinement (BPR) module that refines the pose using appearance consistency and centroid alignment constraints. Leveraging GS-RealBlur, we construct a high-quality and diverse dataset. Extensive experiments demonstrate that a deblurring model trained on our dataset achieves superior generalization performance across various real-world deblurring benchmarks, consistently outperforming models trained on existing synthetic and real-world datasets. The code and dataset will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [8] [Explicit Over Implicit: Enhancing CNNs Via Complex Structure Tensor Representations for Periocular Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15410)
*Kevin Hernandez-Diaz,Josef Bigun,Fernando Alonso-Fernandez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出使用复数结构张量（包含方向特征与置信度）作为CNN输入，可有效克服CNN在提取方向特征方面的局限性，在Open-World和Close-World场景下的眼部图像识别任务中实现5-26%的等错误率降低，且结合小型复数卷积网络可超越主流全尺寸CNN架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CNN在提取方向特征方面存在固有局限性，而方向特征是生物视觉系统中的关键先验信息。作者受哺乳动物视觉系统中方向特征前置处理的策略启发，探索如何通过显式引入方向先验来弥补CNN的表示能力不足。

Method: 将复数结构张量（包含紧凑的方向特征及其置信度）作为CNN的输入，代替传统的灰度图像输入。使用小型复数卷积网络（mini-complex convnets）生成该输入特征，并在六种CNN架构上验证，在Cross-Eyed和PolyU两个公开眼部数据集上进行Close-World和Open-World场景下的生物识别与验证实验。

Result: 相比单独使用灰度图像输入，复数结构张量输入一致性地提高了识别准确率；在Cross-Eyed和PolyU数据集上实现5-26%的等错误率（EER）降低；结合小型复数卷积网络的方式可超越全尺寸主流CNN架构的性能。

Conclusion: 显式方向先验能够有效缓解CNN的表示能力限制，在Open-World和Close-World场景下均有效；该策略不仅提升了CNN的可解释性，也使其更适用于薄客户端（thin-clients）等轻量级部署场景，验证了哺乳动物视觉系统中方向特征前置策略在人工神经网络中的有效性。

Abstract: Our study provides evidence that CNNs struggle to extract orientation features effectively. We show that using the Complex Structure Tensor, which contains compact orientation features with certainties, as input to CNNs consistently improves identification accuracy compared to grayscale inputs alone. Experiments also demonstrated that our inputs, provided by mini-complex convnets, combined with reduced CNN sizes, outperformed full-fledged, prevailing CNN architectures. This suggests that the upfront use of orientation features in CNNs, a strategy seen in mammalian vision, not only mitigates their limitations but also enhances their explainability and relevance to thin-clients. Experiments were conducted on publicly available datasets comprising periocular images (Cross-Eyed and PolyU) for biometric identification and verification in both Close-World and Open-World Scenarios using six CNN architectures. Our experiments on the Cross-Eyed and PolyU datasets yield a 5-26% reduction in EER, providing strong empirical evidence that explicit orientation priors mitigate CNN representational limits in Open-World and Close-World scenarios.

</details>


### [9] [Dataset-Origin Signatures and Shortcut Learning in Screening Mammography AI: A Cross-Dataset Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15416)
*Parham Hajishafiezahramini,Matthew Hamilton,Oscar Meruvia-Pastor,Edward Kendall*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在筛查性乳腺X光摄影AI中，使用外部活检确诊数据集补充训练数据反而因域偏移导致性能下降（AUC-ROC从0.737降至0.620-0.644），数据集来源预测实验证实了域特异性特征的主导作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 筛查性乳腺X光摄影的AI需要代表低癌症患病率和细微异常的训练数据，但这类数据获取困难。研究者想探究是否可以通过补充来自异常富集的外部数据集（活检确诊病例）来提升模型性能。

Method: 使用NLBSD（筛查数据集）为基础，补充CBIS-DDSM和CMMD（外部异常富集数据集），采用Mammo-CLIP初始化的EfficientNet-B5编码器作为冻结线性探针，在一致的预处理和患者级数据划分下进行评估。同时设计诊断实验，将任务重构为预测每个检查的数据集来源。

Result: NLBSD-only模型AUC-ROC为0.737；任何外部数据补充都导致性能显著下降（AUC-ROC 0.620-0.644，p<0.05），且随着引入更多外部源，性能下降加剧。域匹配评估仅在训练和测试域一致时有适度提升。数据集来源预测几乎完美分离，说明数据集特异性特征主导了学习表示。

Conclusion: 简单混合异常富集的外部数据集会引入域偏移，其负面影响超过增加阳性病例带来的益处，需要设计域感知策略来合并异质乳腺X光数据集。

Abstract: Reliable AI for screening mammography requires training data representative of the low cancer prevalence and subtle abnormalities found in screening populations. We examined whether supplementing such data with biopsy-confirmed cases from abnormal-enriched external datasets improves performance. Using the Newfoundland and Labrador Breast Screening Dataset (NLBSD) alongside CBIS-DDSM and CMMD, we evaluated an EfficientNet-B5 encoder initialized with Mammo-CLIP weights as a frozen linear probe under consistent preprocessing and patient-level splits.
  The NLBSD-only model achieved an AUC-ROC of 0.737 (95% CI [0.686, 0.785]). Adding external positive cases reduced performance in every configuration (AUC-ROC = 0.620--0.644; DeLong test, Holm-corrected $p < 0.05$), with degradation increasing as additional sources were introduced. Domain-matched evaluation produced modest gains only when the training and test domains coincided, and no configuration surpassed the NLBSD-only model.
  As a diagnostic, we reframed the task as predicting each examination's dataset of origin. The datasets were separated almost perfectly despite identical preprocessing, indicating that dataset-specific characteristics strongly influence the learned representation. These findings show that naïvely pooling abnormal-enriched mammography datasets can introduce domain shift that outweighs the benefit of additional positive cases. Differences in acquisition, intensity mapping, and dataset construction persist after normalization, motivating domain-aware strategies for combining heterogeneous mammography datasets.

</details>


### [10] [Trajectory-aware Cross-view Geo-localization with Sequential Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15491)
*Tianyi Gao,Jiayu Lin,Danielle Beaulieu,Nathan Jacobs*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出跨视角地理定位中融合视频和路线描述两种轨迹模态的方法，构建了SeqGeo-VL多模态数据集和TrajLoc统一框架，通过TrajMod模块实现轨迹几何感知的表示学习，在视频和文本地理定位任务上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨视角地理定位方法虽利用视频序列丰富时空线索，但忽略了另一种互补的序列模态——路线描述（路线描述能以更高抽象层次捕捉相同轨迹，且常是唯一可用输入，如用户引导自动驾驶车辆到接驳点），因此需弥合这一差距。

Method: 1) 构建SeqGeo-VL数据集（~39K视频-文本-卫星三元组）；2) 提出TrajLoc统一框架，同时处理视频片段和路线描述，利用密集视觉语义和抽象语言语义实现跨视图匹配的相互增强；3) 提出TrajMod轻量模块，将查询嵌入条件化于轨迹几何信息，生成空间感知表示。

Result: 在视频和文本地理定位任务上均显著超越现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 融合视频和路线描述两种轨迹模态能有效提升跨视角地理定位性能，TrajMod的轨迹几何感知机制是关键创新点。

Abstract: Cross-view geo-localization matches ground-level observations against geo-tagged satellite imagery. Recent methods show that sequential queries such as video clips yield richer spatiotemporal cues than single images, yet they overlook a complementary sequential modality: route descriptions -- which capture the same trajectory at a higher level of abstraction and are often the only input available (e.g., a user directing an autonomous vehicle to a pickup point). To bridge this gap, we introduce SeqGeo-VL, a dataset of $\sim$39K video-text-satellite triplets, and TrajLoc, a unified framework capable of processing both video clips and route descriptions. By leveraging both dense visual and abstract linguistic semantics, TrajLoc enables these modalities to mutually reinforce cross-view matching. We further propose TrajMod, a lightweight module that conditions query embeddings on trajectory geometry, yielding spatially-aware representations. Experiments show that TrajLoc achieves substantial gains over state-of-the-art methods on both video and text geo-localization. The project page is available at https://humblegamer.github.io/trajloc/.

</details>


### [11] [LLM-Driven AutoML for Cross-Lingual Handwritten OCR: Closed-Loop Neural Architecture Search with GPT-5, GPT-4o, and Claude Sonnet 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15509)
*Mobina Kashaniyan,Amirhossein Ghassemi,Nasser Mozayani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一个完全自动化的闭环AutoML框架，利用GPT-5、GPT-4o和Claude Sonnet 4作为自主神经架构设计器，在270次实验中实现跨语言手写光学字符识别，平均准确率超93%，最高达98.1%，推理延迟41-44毫秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统手写识别依赖人工架构设计、领域特定预处理和超参数调优，缺乏自动化和跨语言泛化能力，且难以兼顾准确率与计算效率。

Method: 让多个大型语言模型（GPT-5、GPT-4o、Claude Sonnet 4）作为自治神经架构设计器，独立完成神经网络架构的生成、训练、评估和迭代优化，利用先前试验的性能反馈进行闭环自改进，无需人工干预。

Result: 在阿拉伯语、波斯语和英语手写数据集上的270次独立实验中，生成的模型平均测试准确率超93%，最佳准确率达98.1%，推理延迟稳定在41-44毫秒之间。

Conclusion: 大型语言模型可作为有效的AutoML智能体进行神经架构搜索，实现可扩展、适应不同文字且可复现的跨语言手写识别。

Abstract: We present a fully automated closed-loop AutoML framework that uses GPT-5, GPT-4o, and Claude Sonnet 4 as autonomous neural architecture designers for cross-lingual handwritten optical character recognition. Each large language model independently generates, trains, evaluates, and iteratively refines neural network architectures using performance feedback from previous trials. The framework is evaluated on Arabic, Persian, and English handwriting datasets through 270 independent experiments. It consistently discovers accurate and computationally efficient models without manual architecture design, domain-specific preprocessing, or hyperparameter tuning. The generated models achieve mean test accuracies above 93 percent, a best accuracy of 98.1 percent, and inference latency between 41 and 44 milliseconds. The results demonstrate that large language models can function as effective AutoML agents for neural architecture search, enabling scalable, script-adaptive, and reproducible handwriting recognition across languages.

</details>


### [12] [Intentional Electromagnetic Interference Attacks on Facial Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15512)
*Tyler Fitzsimmons,Adam Czajka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了生物识别传感器物理到数字管道中的关键漏洞，证明使用常见射频设备即可实施有意电磁干扰攻击，评估了先进人脸识别方法对此类攻击的鲁棒性，并创建了含电磁干扰干扰的人脸图像数据集作为新基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机视觉攻击多集中于数字域，而生物识别领域（尤其是人脸识别）面临物理呈现攻击的威胁，但针对传感器硬件层面的电磁干扰攻击研究不足，缺乏标准化测试方法。

Method: (a) 使用常见射频设备实施有意电磁干扰；(b) 评估先进人脸识别方法对射频攻击的鲁棒性；(c) 构建含电磁干扰干扰的人脸图像数据集作为基准。

Result: 证明了射频电磁干扰可成功干扰人脸识别传感器，揭示了现有先进人脸识别方法对此类攻击的脆弱性，并提供了标准化测试数据集。

Conclusion: 电磁干扰攻击是对现有呈现攻击范式的补充与拓展，可纳入ISO/IEC 30107标准体系，为人脸识别系统硬件层面的安全性测试提供新方向。

Abstract: Attacks on general computer vision algorithms are often relegated to the digital domain, with the optimization performed purely in the digital world and then translated to physical mediums for implementation. In the field of biometrics, including facial recognition, physical presentation attacks targeting biometric sensors are dominant and present significant opportunity and risk. This paper highlights a critical vulnerability in the physical-to-digital pipeline of biometric sensors and provides a standardized approach for testing facial recognition system robustness against hardware attacks, going beyond and potentially complementing presentation attacks (as defined in ISO/IEC 30107 standard series). Specifically, in this work we (a) demonstrate that intentional electromagnetic interference is possible to be conducted with commonly accessible radio frequency (RF) equipment, (b) assess the robustness of state-of-the-art face recognition methods against RF-based attacks, and (c) provide a dataset composed of face images captured with and without electromagnetic interference to serve as a new benchmark for testing modern face matchers against RF-sourced interference.

</details>


### [13] [SLAPBench: Benchmarking Multimodal Large Language Models for Four-Finger SLAP Fingerprint Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15517)
*Bibesh Pyakurel,M. G. Sarwar Murshed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 首次提出基于多模态大语言模型(MLLM)的四指SLAP指纹验证基准SLAPBench，发现提示策略控制模型崩溃行为，模型能力决定区分性能，其中Qwen3-VL-8B在相似度评分提示下达到完美AUC=1.000，但可能源于近重复检测而非真正能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尚无基准评估多模态大语言模型能否从SLAP图像中验证身份，因此需要建立首个针对MLLM的四指SLAP指纹验证基准。

Method: 基于NIST SD302b构建包含7,832对(176匹配, 7,656非匹配)的SLAPBench基准，在零样本、任务描述和相似度评分三种提示下评估InternVL3-8B、Qwen2.5-VL-7B、Qwen3-VL-8B、Gemma-3-12B和Claude Opus 4.8五个MLLM。

Result: 任务描述提示使开源模型FAR接近100%；Claude Opus 4.8最佳二进制结果FAR=20.2%；相似度评分消除崩溃，Claude AUC=0.953，Gemma-3-12B AUC=0.837，InternVL3-8B倒置AUC=0.590，Qwen2.5-VL-7B接近随机AUC=0.567，Qwen3-VL-8B完美AUC=1.000；同分辨率控制实验排除分辨率捷径，但无法排除近重复检测。

Conclusion: SLAPBench建立了首个SLAP专用MLLM基线，证明提示策略控制崩溃行为，模型能力控制区分性能；Qwen3-VL-8B的完美表现需谨慎对待，可能来自数据集的近重复检测问题。

Abstract: Four-finger SLAP fingerprints are flat live-scan impressions of the index, middle, ring, and little fingers of one hand, used for identity verification in border control and law enforcement. No benchmark has evaluated whether multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can verify identity from SLAP images. We introduce SLAPBench, the first benchmark for MLLM-based four-finger SLAP fingerprint verification, built from NIST SD302b with 7,832 pairs (176 mated, 7,656 non-mated). We evaluate four open-source MLLMs (InternVL3-8B, Qwen2.5-VL-7B, Qwen3-VL-8B, Gemma-3-12B) and the proprietary Claude Opus 4.8 under zero-shot, task-description, and similarity-scoring prompts.
  Prompting governs verification behavior. Task-description prompting collapses all four open-source models to near-100% False Accept Rate (FAR), and Gemma-3-12B collapses under zero-shot as well; Claude Opus 4.8 alone resists collapse under both binary prompts, giving the best binary result (FAR = 20.2%). Similarity scoring removes collapse across the open-source models and exposes wide capability gaps: Claude reaches AUC = 0.953 and Gemma-3-12B 0.837, while InternVL3-8B is inverted (AUC = 0.590) and Qwen2.5-VL-7B near random (0.567). Qwen3-VL-8B attains perfect separation (AUC = 1.000), which we treat as a diagnostic rather than as capability: SD302b holds one SLAP capture per finger position, so mated pairs are cross-resolution. A matched-resolution control leaves the perfect score intact, ruling out the resolution shortcut; what cannot be excluded within SD302b is near-duplicate detection, since a mated pair is one capture rendered twice. A fairness probe over gender, race, and age suggests disparity grows as discrimination weakens. SLAPBench establishes the first SLAP-specific MLLM baseline and shows that prompting governs collapse while model capability governs discrimination.

</details>


### [14] [Physics-aware Masked Diffusion-based Flood Simulation for Urban Fisheye Disaster Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15527)
*Sodtavilan Odonchimed,Tsogt Enkhbayar,Oyunzul Munkhtamga,Munkhjargal Gochoo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PhysFlood系统，利用扩散模型从单张鱼眼镜头图像合成逼真的洪水场景，支持通过水位等物理变量自由控制生成多样化洪水模拟，在定性人类研究中验证了真实性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市灾害物理模拟对防灾和异常检测至关重要，但真实洪水数据严重短缺，且鱼眼镜头图像存在固有畸变，导致高精度模拟面临挑战。

Method: 基于扩散模型（Diffusion Models）的物理仿真系统PhysFlood，从单张鱼眼镜头图像合成逼真洪水，支持通过水位等物理意义变量自由控制生成多样化的洪水场景。

Result: 定性人类研究表明，PhysFlood生成的模拟图像在真实性和鲁棒性方面均达到可接受水平。

Conclusion: PhysFlood系统能够从单张鱼眼图像有效合成逼真的洪水场景，为城市洪水模拟和防灾提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Physical simulations that predict the behavior of urban disasters, such as climate-related flooding, play a crucial role in disaster prevention and the development of anomaly detection models. However, the severe shortage of flood data in real-world environments, combined with the inherent distortions of fisheye lens images, which are used for urban surveillance, has made high-precision simulations challenging. To address this, we propose a new physical simulation system PhysFlood that leverages Diffusion Models to synthesize realistic floods from just a single image captured by a fisheye lens. Our system not only enables simulation from a single image, but also features the ability to freely control and generate diverse flood scenarios by manipulating physically meaningful variables, such as water levels. In our evaluation experiments, we conducted a qualitative human study and demonstrated that the simulation images generated by PhysFlood exhibit both acceptable realism and robustness.

</details>


### [15] [E3DGS: Unified Geometric-Photometric Equivariance for 3D Gaussian Splatting via Color-as-Geometry Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15536)
*Chankyo Kim,Maani Ghaffari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对3D高斯泼溅中几何与外观在SE(3)变换下的对称性不匹配问题，提出基于表示论将光度学（球谐系数）代数同构为秩2几何张量，构建统一矩阵嵌入（Unified Matrix Embedding）将全部高斯属性提升至gl(3)载波空间，进而设计出无需Clebsch-Gordan张量积的刚性等变架构E3DGS，在目标视觉和动作条件高斯世界建模中展现出更强的相机帧鲁棒性和数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS方法在SE(3)等变架构上面临表示瓶颈：颜色被视为信号而非几何实体，导致几何（位置、协方差）与外观（球谐系数）在相机帧变化下难以统一对称性。旋转对不同属性作用各异（μ→Rμ, Σ→RΣR^T, f_ℓ→D^ℓ(R)f_ℓ），这种异构性使得严格等变困难，现有方法要么丢弃要么展平SH系数，从而破环了对称性。

Method: 基于表示论证明：对于ℓ≤2的球谐阶数，光度学与秩2几何张量代数同构，Wigner-D作用可精确重构为3×3矩阵的共轭作用。据此提出统一矩阵嵌入（Unified Matrix Embedding），将所有高斯属性提升到gl(3)统一载波空间。基于"Color-as-Geometry"范式，设计E3DGS——一种无需Clebsch-Gordan张量积的SE(3)等变架构。

Result: 在目标视觉和动作条件高斯世界建模任务上，统一方法在相机帧变化下展现出强鲁棒性，并提升了数据效率。

Conclusion: 通过将颜色重新解释为几何实体，实现了3D高斯属性在SE(3)下的统一等变处理，无需复杂的张量积运算即可获得鲁棒性和数据效率的提升。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) captures scenes by coupling explicit geometry (position, covariance) with view-dependent photometry (Spherical Harmonics). However, building $\mathrm{SE}(3)$-equivariant architectures on these primitives presents a fundamental representation bottleneck. Color has been treated as a signal rather than a geometric entity, making it nontrivial to unify symmetry across geometry and appearance as the camera frame changes. While translations are handled by relative coordinates, rotations act heterogeneously across attributes: $μ\mapsto Rμ$, $Σ\mapsto RΣR^\top$, and $f_\ell\mapsto D^\ell(R)f_\ell$. This mismatch complicates strict equivariance, leading existing methods to either discard or flatten SH coefficients, thereby breaking symmetry. We propose a unified solution rooted in representation theory: for SH degrees $\ell\le2$, photometry is algebraically isomorphic to a rank-2 geometric tensor. We prove that the Wigner-$D$ action on these SH coefficients can be exactly reformulated as the conjugation action on $3\times3$ matrices. Leveraging this, we introduce the Unified Matrix Embedding, a lifting that maps all Gaussian attributes into a unified carrier space, $\mathfrak{gl}(3)$. Building on the "Color-as-Geometry" formulation, we present E3DGS, a rigid-body ($\mathrm{SE}(3)$) equivariant architecture that processes 3D Gaussians without Clebsch-Gordan tensor products. Evaluations on object vision and action-conditioned Gaussian world modeling demonstrate that our unified approach yields strong robustness under camera-frame changes and improved data efficiency.

</details>


### [16] [ImprovedVBGS: Real-time Continual Variational Bayes Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15542)
*Damani Mguni-Coker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ImprovedVBGS通过空间截断变分推断和改进的重新分配机制，将逐帧延迟从约84秒降至0.05秒（加速1680倍），同时保持重建质量，实现了满足实时性要求的动态高斯溅射持续学习框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学和自主导航中的许多应用需要实时（on-the-fly）重建能力。现有VBGS方法虽能实现无需回放缓冲区的持续学习，但其对已观测点的逐帧迭代导致延迟过高（约84秒/帧），无法满足严格的实时性、内存和延迟要求。

Method: (i) 空间截断变分推断（Spatially Truncated Variational Inference）：限制变分更新的空间范围，避免全局迭代；(ii) 改进的重新分配机制（Improved Reassignment）：利用前向传播、截断策略并消除浪费的动态重新编译开销。

Result: 在NeRF合成数据集上，RTX 3070 Ti显卡的逐帧平均延迟从约84.0秒降至约0.050秒，实现1680倍加速，且重建质量保持不变。

Conclusion: ImprovedVBGS成功解决了VBGS在实时持续重建中的瓶颈问题，通过两项关键技术创新大幅加速了逐帧处理速度，使其适用于对延迟敏感的机器人学和自主导航场景。

Abstract: On-the-fly reconstruction is a key requirement for many applications in robotics and autonomous navigation. Variational Bayes Gaussian Splatting (VBGS) enables continual learning without replay buffers using Coordinate Ascent Variational Inference (CAVI), but its per-frame iterations over all observed points make it too slow for real-time use with strict memory and latency requirements. We present ImprovedVBGS, an accelerated framework for on-the-fly continual reconstruction. This is achieved primarily through (i) spatially truncated variational inference, and (ii) improved reassignment that uses forwarding, truncation and eliminates wasteful dynamic recompilation. On the NeRF synthetic dataset, we reduce mean per-frame latency from ~84.0 s to ~0.050 s on an RTX 3070 Ti, a 1680x speed-up while maintaining reconstruction quality.

</details>


### [17] [When Can Test-Time Adaptation Help Zero-Shot CT Vision-Language Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15556)
*Ailar Mahdizadeh,Puria Azadi Moghadam,Xiangteng He,Leonid Sigal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了零样本3D CT视觉语言模型在测试时自适应（TTA）中的条件性——只有当体积输入保留编码器深度结构且基础表征可迁移时TTA才有效，并提出了首个针对该场景的多标签TTA方法CARVE（基数感知保留视图熵），通过估计样本特定正标签基数并优化top-k目标，在保持共现异常的同时实现内存高效的多视图自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实CT扫描通常包含多个共现异常，零样本多标签预测在分布偏移下的可靠性尚不明确。现有TTA方法针对的是自然图像或2D医学分割的多类softmax预测，没有方法处理零样本3D CT VLMs的无监督多标签自适应问题。

Method: 首先通过受控诊断分析确定TTA的条件：体积输入需保留编码器深度结构，基础表征需能迁移到目标队列。然后提出CARVE：估计样本特异性正标签基数k̂，优化top-k目标以保留共现异常，通过无梯度评分弱3D视图后保留子集更新，实现内存高效的多视图自适应。

Result: 在对比CT-CLIP和解剖感知fVLM上，当基础模型已经具有判别性时，CARVE在多标签、三分类和二分类CT任务上提供了最一致的改进。深度缩减单独就会使内部AUROC下降超过0.12。

Conclusion: 多标签TTA对零样本3D CT VLMs是一个独特的问题，CARVE作为基数感知解决方案有效应对了这一挑战。

Abstract: 3D CT vision-language models (VLMs) classify abnormalities from text prompts in a zero-shot manner, enabling cross-institution deployment where labels are scarce and clinical tasks shift faster than supervised models can be retrained. A real CT scan, however, typically contains several co-occurring abnormalities, and the reliability of zero-shot multi-label prediction under distribution shift remains poorly understood. Test-time adaptation (TTA) updates a model on unlabeled target scans without source data or target annotations, yet existing TTA methods target multi-class softmax prediction on natural images or 2D medical segmentation, and none addresses unsupervised multi-label adaptation for zero-shot 3D CT VLMs. We study when TTA helps zero-shot 3D CT VLMs. A controlled diagnostic analysis shows that TTA is conditional: the volumetric input must preserve the encoder's depth structure, and the base representation must transfer to the target cohort, with depth reduction alone lowering internal AUROC by more than 0.12. We then focus on the regime where the base model already separates present from absent abnormalities. We introduce CARVE (Cardinality-Aware Retained-View Entropy), the first TTA method for this setting. CARVE estimates a sample-specific positive-label cardinality $\hat{k}$, optimizes a top-$\hat{k}$ objective to preserve co-occurring abnormalities, and performs memory-efficient multi-view adaptation by scoring weak 3D views without gradients before updating on a retained subset. Across contrastive CT-CLIP and anatomy-aware fVLM, CARVE provides the most consistent improvements across multi-label, three-class, and binary CT tasks when the base model is already discriminative. These results establish multi-label TTA for zero-shot 3D CT VLMs as a distinct problem and CARVE as a cardinality-aware solution.

</details>


### [18] [Are All Tokens Necessary for Visual Place Recognition? An Empirical Study of Token Reduction for Efficient Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15563)
*Tong Jin,Yunpeng Liu,Shuyu Hu,Qinghua Zhang,Ruize Han,Song Wang,Feng Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地研究了视觉标记压缩（token reduction）在视觉位置识别（VPR）中的应用，通过基准测试评估了多种标记剪枝、合并及混合方法在多个VPR模型和数据集上的效果，发现标记压缩可在保持识别精度下降<1%的前提下，降低最多29%的计算成本和提升最多44%的吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉变换器（尤其是基础模型）的VPR方法虽然识别性能优异，但处理所有视觉标记导致计算开销巨大，阻碍了在实时和资源受限场景中的部署。因此，作者提出质疑：是否所有视觉标记对于VPR都是必要的？并以此出发进行系统性研究。

Method: 构建了首个针对VPR的标记压缩系统基准测试，涵盖三类代表性方法（标记剪枝、标记合并、剪枝-合并混合），在多个最先进的VPR模型和覆盖城市、郊区和自然环境的多类基准数据集上进行评估，并从识别性能、计算复杂度、推理速度、定性可视化和边缘设备部署效率等多个角度进行分析。

Result: 标记压缩可降低最多29%的计算成本，提升最多44%的吞吐量，同时识别精度下降不超过1%。揭示了标记压缩在VPR中的多个重要特性，并提供了准确性与推理效率之间权衡的实用见解。

Conclusion: 该工作为未来标记高效的VPR和高效视觉检索系统研究建立了全面的基础，代码和模型将开源。

Abstract: Recent visual place recognition (VPR) methods based on vision transformers, particularly foundation models, have achieved remarkable recognition performance. However, these models process all visual tokens throughout the entire network, resulting in substantial computational overhead, which hinders their deployment in real-time and resource-constrained scenarios. A natural question thus arises: are all visual tokens necessary for VPR? To answer this question, we present the first systematic benchmark of token reduction for efficient visual place recognition. Our benchmark comprehensively evaluates representative token pruning, token merging, and hybrid pruning-merging methods across multiple state-of-the-art VPR models and diverse benchmark datasets covering urban, suburban, and natural environments. We further investigate token reduction from multiple perspectives, including recognition performance under different reduction configurations, computational complexity, inference speed, qualitative visualization, and deployment efficiency on edge devices. Through extensive experiments and in-depth analysis, our benchmark reveals multiple important characteristics of token reduction in VPR and provides several practical insights into the trade-offs between accuracy and inference efficiency. For example, token reduction can reduce computational cost by up to 29\% and improve throughput by up to 44\%, while incurring less than 1\% degradation in recognition accuracy. Overall, this work establishes a comprehensive foundation for future research on token-efficient VPR and efficient visual retrieval systems. Our codes and models will be available at https://github.com/Tong-Jin01/TokenReduction4VPR

</details>


### [19] [Ask Twice, Look Twice: Prompt Echoing Resolves the Question-First Paradox in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15565)
*Rakshanda Hassan Abhinandan,John Galeotti,Deva Ramanan,Gautam Rajendrakumar Gare*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在视觉语言模型(VLM)中，将问题放在图像前会引导感知但难以被答案token关注；放在图像后虽能确保问题被读取但失去感知引导作用。这种矛盾导致"问题在前"策略表现不佳。无需训练的"问题回响"(在图像前后各放一次问题)能同时实现感知引导和问题读取，显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现VLM中问题放置顺序存在悖论：直觉上问题应先于图像，但实际评估中"问题在前"始终不如"图像在前"的表现。作者试图解释这一矛盾现象并寻找解决方案。

Method: 使用Logit-lens和注意力探针分析VLM内部计算机制，通过因果注意力敲除实验验证假设，最终提出"问题回响"(question echoing)方法——在图像前后各放置一次问题，无需训练或架构修改。

Result: 问题回响在NaturalBench、POPE、Winoground和开放VQAv2上均超越最优单一顺序策略，Winoground分组准确率提升高达19个点，消除了最高达17.5个分组准确率点的性能损失。

Conclusion: VLM中问题放置存在感知引导与问题可读性之间的权衡，问题回响通过提示设计即可解决这一矛盾，且与五十年前人类"附加问题"研究中的发现一致。

Abstract: Where should the question go in a vision-language model (VLM) prompt: before the image or after it? Intuition says before: knowing what is asked should tell the model where to look. Yet across visual question answering benchmarks, question-first prompting consistently underperforms the image-first ordering recommended for frontier VLMs, a phenomenon we term the question-first paradox. We trace the paradox to a conflict between two stages of VLM computation. Logit-lens and attention probes show the intuition is half right: a question placed before the image genuinely steers perception, moving image patch representations toward question-relevant concepts. The failure lies downstream. Stranded behind hundreds of image tokens, the question is barely attended by the answer token, which instead commits to image-driven (often wrong) answers; a causal attention knockout confirms that the answer reads the question only when the question follows the image. The diagnosis yields a training-free fix: question echoing, restating the question on both sides of the image so that one copy steers perception while the other is read out at answer time. The same division of labor appears in a fifty-year-old finding on human ``adjunct questions'', where repeating a question before and after a passage aids comprehension more than either position alone. Echoing the image as well brings further gains, restoring the whole-image view a causal decoder otherwise loses. The paradox holds across five open VLMs, costing up to 17.5 group-accuracy points. Echoed prompts close it and surpass the best single-pass ordering on NaturalBench, POPE, Winoground, and open-ended VQAv2, by up to 19 Winoground group-accuracy points, with no training, fine-tuning, or architecture change. The paradox reveals a trade-off between steering perception and preserving question access; echoing resolves it through prompt design alone.

</details>


### [20] [Geometric Distillation from Rectified Stereo: Leveraging Epipolar Cues for Monocular Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15600)
*Jung-Hee Kim,Xiaoming Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EpiDistill框架，通过极线蒸馏将多视图模型的尺度感知几何先验迁移到单目深度基础模型中，在不依赖多视图输入的情况下显著提升零样本度量深度估计性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目深度基础模型在多样化环境中表现出色，但受限于单视图推理固有的尺度模糊性，导致度量深度估计时尺度预测错位；而多视图模型虽能学习鲁棒的场景级几何和一致尺度，但其优势在单图像推理时因缺乏显式几何约束而消失。为弥合这一差距，需要将多视图模型的尺度感知几何先验迁移到单目模型中。

Method: 提出Epipolar Distillation (EpiDistill)框架，利用Rectified Stereo Tokens实现极线蒸馏，使单视图预测模型在推理时不需多视图输入，仍能保留极线注意力模式并维持几何一致性。

Result: 该方法显著提升了零样本度量深度估计性能，尤其在ETH3D和DIODE等对尺度对齐要求严苛的数据集上表现突出；且方法为模型无关的，能一致提升UniDepthV2和DepthPro等SOTA ViT模型的性能。

Conclusion: EpiDistill成功弥合了单目与多视图深度估计之间的差距，通过极线蒸馏将多视图的尺度先验高效注入单目模型，实现无需多视图输入的尺度感知单目深度估计。

Abstract: Monocular depth foundation models have demonstrated remarkable generalization capabilities across diverse environments. However, they continue to struggle with metric depth estimation in diverse environments. This limitation stems from the inherent scale ambiguity of single-view inference, leading to misaligned scale predictions even when the relative geometry is accurate. Conversely, recent multi-view foundation models leverage cross-view cues to learn robust scene-level geometry and consistent scale. Yet, these benefits typically vanish during single-image inference, as the absence of explicit geometric constraints causes performance to degrade. To bridge this gap, we propose a novel framework that transfers the scale-aware geometric priors of multi-view models into monocular depth foundation models. Specifically, we introduce an Epipolar Distillation (EpiDistill), an approach utilizing Rectified Stereo Tokens, which enables the single-view prediction model to retain epipolar attention patterns and maintain geometric consistency without requiring multi-view inputs at inference. Experimental results demonstrate that our method significantly improves zero-shot metric depth estimation, particularly on challenging datasets like ETH3D and DIODE where scale alignment is critical. Furthermore, our approach is model-agnostic, consistently boosting the performance of state-of-the-art ViT-based models, including UniDepthV2 and DepthPro.

</details>


### [21] [WREN: Low Light Image Enhancement Using Retinex theory-based Double U-Net-like Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15604)
*Reina Kaneko,Junya Hara,Hiroshi Higashi,Yuichi Tanaka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出WREN，一种基于Retinex理论的双U-Net结构低光图像增强网络，通过分解图像为反射图和光照图并仅增强光照图，结合尺度不变损失函数，实现对不同动态范围场景的鲁棒增强，在多个数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Retinex理论的低光图像增强方法因分解非唯一性问题，在不同动态范围场景下常出现过增强或分解不稳定的问题，缺乏对光照尺度变化的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出WREN双U-Net结构：第一个网络采用单编码器-双解码器结构分解输入图像为反射图和光照图；第二个网络在编码器和解码器之间加入定制化Transformer模块，仅增强光照图，严格遵循Retinex理论假设；最后将增强后的光照图与反射图重组。网络使用尺度不变损失函数进行端到端训练。

Result: 在多个数据集上达到最先进的性能表现。

Conclusion: WREN通过严格遵循Retinex理论的双U-Net设计和尺度不变损失函数，有效解决了不同动态范围场景下的过增强问题，实现了鲁棒的低光图像增强。

Abstract: This paper proposes a neural network for low light image enhancement (LLIE) based on retinex theory to make LLIE robust for various dynamic range scenes. The retinex theory is an image formulation model inspired by a human color perception hypothesis, where a low light image is decomposed into intrinsic color context (i.e., reflectance map) and scene-dependent illumination (i.e., illumination map). Due to non-uniqueness of its decomposition, existing retinex-based LLIE methods often fail to achieve stable decomposition, which lead to over-enhancement. Typically, they are sensitive to the dynamic ranges that vary in different lighting conditions. To tackle this issue, we propose WREN: An LLIE neural network with double U-Net-like structures. WREN consists of two U-Net-like sub-networks. The first network has one encoder and two decoders that decompose an input image into the reflectance and illumination maps. The second network with a customized Transformer block between an encoder and a decoder only enhances the illumination map obtained from the first network: This completely follows the assumption of the retinex theory. Finally, the enhanced illumination map is recombined with the reflectance map. The network is trained end-to-end with a scale-invariant loss function, which gives robustness against the illumination scaling. Numerical results show that our method achieves the state-of-the-art performance across multiple datasets. Our code is available online.

</details>


### [22] [Benchmarking MRI Representations for Deep Learning-Based Focal Cortical Dysplasia Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15605)
*Soumen Ghosh,John Phamnguyen,Amit Soni Arya,Subhojit Mandal,Tilottama Goswami,Rajat Vashistha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了不同MRI表征（T1w、FLAIR、比值衍生图及其多模态组合）对基于nnU-Net框架的局灶性皮质发育不良（FCD）自动分割性能的影响。在85名FCD患者和25名健康对照的公开数据集中，四通道多模态配置（T1w+FLAIR+比值图）取得了最优的Dice分数（0.376），相较于传统T1w+FLAIR组合提高了5.0%。结果表明，MRI表征设计是影响深度学习FCD分割性能的重要因素，应与网络架构共同优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FCD是药物难治性局灶性癫痫的主要结构性病因之一，但在常规MRI上其成像特征细微且异质性强，导致准确识别和勾画非常困难。尽管T1w和FLAIR影像已常规用于术前评估，但不同MRI表征对深度学习FCD分割的具体贡献尚不清楚，这一缺口促使作者开展系统性基准研究。

Method: 使用nnU-Net框架，基于包含85名FCD患者和25名健康对照的公开术前MRI数据集，评估八种输入配置（包括T1w、FLAIR、比值衍生图及其多模态组合）。所有实验保持相同的预处理、网络架构、优化策略和五折交叉验证，以隔离MRI表征的单一影响。

Result: 单模态中FLAIR表现最佳，比值衍生图单独不足以可靠识别细微FCD。将比值图与T1w和FLAIR结合能持续改善病灶勾画，四通道多模态配置（T1w+FLAIR+两种比值图）取得最高总体Dice分数（0.376），较传统T1w+FLAIR组合相对提升5.0%。

Conclusion: MRI表征设计是深度学习FCD分割中重要但尚未充分探索的环节，应与网络架构同步优化，以进一步提升分割性能。

Abstract: Focal cortical dysplasia (FCD) is one of the leading structural causes of drug-resistant focal epilepsy, yet its subtle and heterogeneous imaging characteristics make accurate identification and delineation challenging on conventional magnetic resonance imaging (MRI). Although T1-weighted (T1w) and fluid-attenuated inversion recovery (FLAIR) images are routinely acquired for presurgical evaluation, the contribution of different MRI representations to deep learning-based FCD segmentation remains poorly understood. In this study, we present a systematic benchmark of MRI representations for automated FCD segmentation using the nnU-Net framework. A publicly available presurgical MRI dataset comprising 85 FCD subjects and 25 healthy controls was used to evaluate eight input configurations, including conventional MRI contrasts (T1w and FLAIR), ratio-derived representations, and their multimodal combinations. To isolate the effect of MRI representation, all experiments employed identical preprocessing, network architecture, optimization strategy, and five-fold cross-validation. Among the evaluated single-modality representations, FLAIR achieved the strongest overall performance, whereas ratio-derived representations alone were insufficient for reliable identification of subtle FCD. Incorporating ratio-derived representations with conventional T1w and FLAIR images consistently improved lesion delineation, with the four-channel multimodal configuration achieving the highest overall Dice score (0.376), representing a 5.0% relative improvement over the conventional T1w+FLAIR representation. These findings demonstrate that MRI representation design is an important yet underexplored component of deep learning-based FCD segmentation and should be optimized alongside network architecture.

</details>


### [23] [Region-Grounded Vision-Language Learning for Detection-Guided Mammographic Lesion Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15615)
*Zhengbo Zhou,Jiren Li,Dooman Arefan,Margarita Zuley,Shandong Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种区域级视觉-语言对比学习方法（Region-Grounded VLL），通过病灶区域与结构化临床描述的对比预训练，结合辅助检测头与对比分类联合优化，解决乳腺X线影像中全局图文对齐不适用的问题，在病灶分类上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统全局图像-文本对比学习不适用于乳腺X线影像，因为诊断相关的病灶是空间局部化的，仅占图像的小部分区域，关键的形态学线索在全图级表示学习中被稀释。

Method: 1）区域-文本对比预训练：将病灶区域特征与放射学元数据中结构化临床描述进行对齐；引入多目标损失函数，包含正对齐、细粒度语义难负例和背景抑制，以缓解低词汇量下的语义坍缩和背景偏置。2）联合优化：辅助病灶检测头与对比分类损失同时优化，保持空间敏感性，实现定位感知的恶性分类。

Result: 在CBIS-DDSM和VinDr-Mammo两个独立数据集上，在域内、跨数据集和迁移学习设置下均优于相关对比方法。

Conclusion: 区域级视觉-语言对比学习能更好地模拟放射科医生的诊断范式，通过空间局部化对齐和检测引导的分类，有效提升乳腺病灶恶性分类性能。

Abstract: Vision-language models trained with contrastive objectives have shown promise in medical image analysis. However, conventional global image-text alignment is ill-suited for mammography, where diagnostically relevant lesions are spatially localized and occupy only a small fraction of the image. Subtle morphological cues critical for malignancy assessment can be diluted when representations are learned at the whole-image level. In this work, we propose a novel region-grounded vision-language learning method for detection-guided mammographic lesion classification. The method mirrors radiologists' diagnostic paradigm. First, a region-text contrastive pretraining stage aligns lesion-specific features with structured clinical descriptors derived from radiology metadata. To mitigate semantic collapse and background bias in low-vocabulary settings, we introduce a multi-component objective incorporating positive alignment, fine-grained semantic hard negatives, and background suppression. Second, an auxiliary lesion detection head is jointly optimized with contrastive classification to preserve spatial sensitivity and enable localization-aware malignancy classification. Extensive experiments on two independent datasets, CBIS-DDSM and VinDr-Mammo, show superior performance of our method compared to related methods under in-domain, cross-dataset, and transfer learning settings.

</details>


### [24] [StructGen: Disambiguating Multi-Reference Image Generation via Structured Context Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15619)
*Jianing Peng,Mengyu Wang,Henghui Ding,Zixiang Li,Ting Liu,Xiaochao Qu,Luoqi Liu,Yao Zhao,Yunchao Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StructGen提出了一种结构化字典格式的多参考图像生成方法，通过显式编码多个参考图像的属性，解决了自然语言指令冗长模糊和多参考数据稀缺的问题，在语义对齐和参考一致性上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多参考图像生成中，现有方法仅依赖自然语言指令，难以精确捕捉复杂的生成意图（如属性与主体的正确关联、空间布局规划），导致语义错位和不一致生成。识别出两个关键限制：自然语言指令冗长模糊，以及高质量多参考数据稀缺。

Method: 采用结构化字典格式编码多个参考图像，实现显式且无歧义的生成意图指定；基于高质量真实图像构建结构化数据集；开发配套训练框架；设计针对多参考场景的专用基准测试。

Result: 在公开基准和提出基准上的大量实验表明，StructGen在语义对齐和参考生成一致性上持续优于现有方法，尤其在包含多个参考的复杂指令场景下优势显著。

Conclusion: 结构化编码格式有效克服了自然语言指令的歧义性和数据稀缺问题，为多参考图像生成提供了一种更精确、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-reference image generation aims to synthesize images by integrating attributes from multiple reference images under textual instructions. As the number of references increases, the task necessitates complex semantic comprehension, such as correctly associating attributes with the intended subjects and planing out coherent spatial arrangement between subjects and their environments. Existing approaches, which rely solely on natural language instruction, often fail to capture these complex intentions precisely, leading to semantic misalignment and inconsistent generation. We identify two key factors behind these limitations: natural language instructions are often verbose and ambiguous, and high-quality multi-reference data is scarce. To address these issues, we propose StructGen, which employs a structured, dictionary-like format to encode multiple reference images, thereby enabling explicit and unambiguous specification of generation intentions. To support this design, we construct a structured dataset based on high-quality real images and develop a corresponding training framework, along with a dedicated benchmark for challenging multi-reference scenarios. Extensive experiments on both public benchmarks and our proposed benchmark demonstrate that StructGen consistently outperforms existing methods on both semantic alignment and detailed reference-generation consistency, especially under complex instructions with multiple references. The code is available at \href{https://jianingpeng0382.github.io/StructGen/}{https://jianingpeng0382.github.io/StructGen/}.

</details>


### [25] [BCG-Former: Toward Pareto-Efficient Hyperspectral Image Classification via Band-Contextual Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15639)
*Gaurav Sharma,Eungjoo Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BCG-Former，一种轻量级CNN-Transformer混合架构，通过波段上下文门控（BCG）、光谱摘要标记和线性注意力机制，在高光谱图像分类中实现精度与计算效率的最佳权衡，在8个基准数据集上以0.10-0.23M参数和亚毫秒级延迟达到91.51%-99.49%的总体精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高光谱图像分类器过度追求精度，忽视了UAV和小型星载传感器等平台的严格计算预算（延迟和内存约束），缺乏面向真实部署场景的轻量化方案。

Method: 提出三项创新：(1) 波段上下文门控（BCG），利用局部波段间上下文和可学习温度锐化实现自适应光谱重校准；(2) 光谱摘要标记，桥接光谱与空间特征；(3) 单遍Band-RoPE结合线性注意力，实现高效的联合表示学习。

Result: 在8个数据集上总体精度91.51%-99.49%，推理延迟0.91-0.95ms，参数量仅0.10-0.23M，始终位于精度-延迟帕累托前沿，在远低于其他方法的计算成本下优于或匹配现有CNN、Transformer和Mamba方法。

Conclusion: BCG-Former是面向实时和大规模遥感应用的强精度-效率帕累托候选方案，消融实验验证了三个组件的互补性，其中BCG贡献最大。

Abstract: Hyperspectral image (HSI) classification systems are increasingly deployed on platforms with strict computational budgets, such as UAVs and small spaceborne sensors. In these settings, accuracy alone is not enough; the model must also run within tight latency and memory constraints. Most recent HSI classifiers, however, focus on accuracy and pay relatively little attention to these constraints. We propose BCG-Former, a lightweight CNN-Transformer hybrid that targets this trade-off. The model introduces three innovations: (1) Band-Contextual Gating (BCG) for adaptive spectral recalibration using local inter-band context and learnable temperature sharpening, (2) a spectral summary token that bridges spectral and spatial features, and (3) single-pass Band-RoPE combined with linear attention for efficient joint representation learning. Evaluated on classical airborne (Pavia University, Salinas, Indian Pines, Houston 2013/2018) and UAV-borne benchmark datasets (WHU-Hi-LongKou, HongHu, and HanChuan), BCG-Former achieves over-all accuracy ranging from 91.51% on Houston 2018 to 99.49% on Houston 2013, while maintaining sub-millisecond inference latency (0.91-0.95ms) and using only 0.10-0.23M parameters. Across all eight benchmarks, BCG-Former consistently resides on or near the Pareto frontier of accuracy versus latency, outperforming or matching recent CNN-, Transformer-, and Mamba-based methods at a fraction of their computational cost. Ablation studies confirm that all three components are complementary, with BCG providing the largest individual contribution. These results establish BCG-Former as a strong accuracy-efficiency Pareto candidate for real-time and large-scale remote sensing applications.

</details>


### [26] [HCIG: A Hierarchical Cross-Modal Incongruity Graph Network for Multimodal Sarcasm and Cyberbullying Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16076)
*Bhavana Verma,Priyanka Meel,Dinesh Kumar Vishwakarma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HCIG和GCCN两种框架，通过层次化跨模态不一致性建模（token、短语、全局三级）和图注意力网络，用于多模态讽刺和网络欺凌检测。HCIG在MMSD上达到85.74%准确率，GCCN在MultiBully上达到68.66% macro-F1，证明层次化多粒度不一致性建模优于传统融合策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态讽刺和网络欺凌检测中，意义往往源于文本与视觉信息之间的不一致性，而非单一模态。现有方法主要依赖特征融合或跨模态注意力，无法有效捕捉不同表征层次上的语义不一致性。

Method: 提出HCIG（层次化跨模态不一致性图网络），在token、短语和全局三个粒度上使用图注意力网络建模跨模态不一致性，并通过层次化注意力机制自适应整合；同时提出GCCN（基于图的跨模态矛盾网络），采用矛盾感知池化进行图推理。

Result: HCIG在MMSD上准确率85.74%、macro-F1 85.29%；GCCN在MultiBully上macro-F1 68.66%；HCIG在MultiBully上准确率69.62%、欺凌类F1 74.90%。

Conclusion: 层次化多粒度不一致性建模比传统融合策略能提供更有效的多模态推理，为社交媒体上的讽刺和网络欺凌检测提供了鲁棒的框架。

Abstract: Multimodal sarcasm and cyberbullying detection remain challenging because the intended meaning often emerges from incongruity between textual and visual information rather than from either modality alone. Existing multimodal approaches primarily rely on feature fusion or cross-modal attention, which may not effectively capture hierarchical semantic inconsistencies across different levels of representation. To address this limitation, this paper proposes HCIG (Hierarchical Cross-modal Incongruity Graph Network), a novel framework that models cross-modal incongruity at token, phrase, and global levels using graph attention networks and adaptively integrates these representations through a learned hierarchical attention mechanism. As a complementary architecture, we also introduce GCCN (Graph-based Cross-modal Contradiction Network), which performs graph-based reasoning using contradiction-aware pooling for efficient multimodal interaction learning. The proposed models are evaluated on the MMSD sarcasm benchmark and the MultiBully cyberbullying dataset, together with comprehensive ablation studies and cross-task transfer experiments. Experimental results demonstrate that HCIG achieves the best performance on MMSD with 85.74% accuracy and 85.29% macro-F1, while GCCN attains the highest macro-F1 (68.66%) on MultiBully and HCIG achieves the highest accuracy (69.62%) and bullying-class F1 (74.90%). The findings demonstrate that hierarchical multi-granularity incongruity modeling provides more effective multimodal reasoning than conventional fusion strategies, offering a robust framework for sarcasm and cyberbullying detection in social media.

</details>


### [27] [DiTango: Cost-Effective Parallel Diffusion Generation with Selective Attention State Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15650)
*Yuyang Chen,Runxin Zhong,Zan Zong,Hengjie Li,Yuyang Jin,Jidong Zhai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DiTango提出了一种基于选择性注意力状态机制的高效并行框架，通过利用上下文并行中序列分区的异质性（空间邻近分区对注意力贡献更大），结合分层通信拓扑和锚点引导的状态规划器，在保持生成质量的同时实现了最高1.9倍端到端和3.2倍注意力加速，并在多节点环境下具有近线性扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散Transformer（DiT）的并行化技术在多节点环境中面临严重的通信开销可扩展性挑战，需要更高效的并行推理方案。

Method: 提出DiTango框架，包括：(1) 选择性注意力状态机制，在去噪步骤间平衡部分注意力计算和历史结果复用；(2) 锚点引导的状态选择规划器，优化每个分区的计算-复用决策；(3) 运行时系统，协调高效的状态中心操作。核心洞察是将上下文并行中序列分区的空间异质性映射到分层通信拓扑，以更低成本访问高贡献分区。

Result: 在主流扩散模型上，DiTango实现了最高1.9倍端到端加速和3.2倍注意力计算加速，多节点设置下具有近线性扩展性，且生成质量与最先进方法相当。

Conclusion: 通过利用序列分区的异质性和设计计算-复用权衡机制，DiTango在显著提升DiT并行推理效率的同时有效保持了生成质量。

Abstract: Recent advances in AI-generated content have driven widespread adoption of Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) for high-resolution, long-duration content generation. While parallelization techniques accelerate diffusion inference, they face significant scalability challenges due to excessive communication overhead in multi-node environments.
  We observe that sequence partitions in Context Parallelism (CP) exhibit distinct heterogeneity: spatially proximate partitions contribute more significantly to attention computation results. By mapping this heterogeneous pattern to hierarchical communication topology, we can access high-contribution partitions with reduced communication cost. This insight motivates our novel selective attention state mechanism that strategically balances partial attention computation and historical result reuse across denoising steps.
  We present DiTango, an efficient parallel framework for DiT generation. DiTango features an anchor-guided state selection planner that optimizes computation-reuse decisions for each partition, complemented by a runtime that orchestrates efficient state-centric operations. This design achieves superior system efficiency while preserving generation quality.
  Experimental evaluation on popular diffusion models demonstrates that DiTango achieves up to 1.9x end-to-end and 3.2x attention speedup with near-linear scaling in multi-node settings, while maintaining generation quality comparable to state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [28] [CSS-BA: Gate-Guided Column Space Search for Bundle Adjustment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15652)
*Ayano Kaneda,Takafumi Taketomi,Shugo Yamaguchi,Shigeo Morishima*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Gate-Guided CSS-BA方法，通过几何约束的低维子空间投影稳定Schur-LM更新，解决低视差/近旋转场景下光束法平差的不适定问题，可作为现有BA管道的即插即用替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Schur-Levenberg-Marquardt在低视差和近旋转场景下病态严重，导致位姿和标定估计不可靠，亟需一种不解耦参数数量、仅约束更新方向的稳定化求解器端改进方案。

Method: 将Column Space Search（CSS）与几何感知门控（geometry-aware gating）集成到Schur-LM框架中，将每次更新约束到几何信息指导的低维子空间；所有相机和点参数保留在优化问题中，仅限制更新方向。

Result: 在通用及弱几何场景下，优化更稳定，相对位姿精度提升，标定行为具有竞争力，且重投影质量保持不变。

Conclusion: Gate-Guided CSS-BA作为求解器侧修改，在不改变经典BA目标和信任域框架的前提下，有效改善了低视差/近旋转场景的稳定性和精度，可作为现有管道的即插即用替代。

Abstract: Bundle adjustment (BA) remains a critical refinement module for image-based 3D reconstruction and continues to improve geometric accuracy even in learning-based pipelines. However, in low-parallax and near-rotational regimes, classical Schur-based Levenberg--Marquardt (LM) often becomes ill-conditioned and yields unreliable pose and calibration estimates. We propose Gate-Guided CSS-BA, a solver-side modification of Schur-LM that preserves the classical BA objective and trust-region framework while constraining each update to a geometrically informed low-dimensional subspace. By integrating Column Space Search (CSS) with geometry-aware gating, the method stabilizes the Schur-LM update without altering the estimation problem. In contrast to keyframe or state-selection approaches, all camera and point parameters remain in the optimization problem; only the update direction is restricted. The method serves as a drop-in replacement for existing BA pipelines. Experiments on both generic and challenging weak-geometry scenarios show more stable optimization, improved relative pose accuracy, and competitive calibration behavior while maintaining reprojection quality.

</details>


### [29] [An Exam for Active Observers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16165)
*Jiarui Zhang,Muzi Tao,Shangshang Wang,Ollie Liu,Xuezhe Ma,Willie Neiswanger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 当前多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在主动视觉观察任务上表现极差。本文提出ActiveVision基准测试，包含17个需要重复视觉感知的任务，顶级模型GPT-5.5仅解决10.6%，Claude Fable 5仅3.5%，而人类平均96.1%。即使允许模型编写和执行视觉代码，差距依然显著，表明MLLMs缺乏闭环主动感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视觉是闭环系统，通过中间假设不断重定向视线，而非单次快照。现有视觉-语言基准无法衡量MLLMs是否具备这种主动观察能力，因此需要设计专门的基准来测试和量化这一能力。

Method: 构建ActiveVision基准，包含3大类17个任务，每个任务强制要求模型进行重复视觉感知而非单次静态描述。评估多个前沿MLLMs（包括GPT-5.5、Claude Fable 5等）的表现，并与人类基线（3名参与者平均96.1%）对比。同时测试模型编写和执行视觉代码的情境。

Result: GPT-5.5在最高推理努力层级仅解决10.6%，其中11/17任务得分为零；Claude Fable 5仅解决3.5%；人类平均96.1%。即使允许模型自写视觉代码，由于代码在真实图像上不可靠且模型缺乏主动感知来捕获代码失败，差距仍然存在。

Conclusion: 当前MLLMs缺乏鲁棒的主动视觉观察能力，这一核心缺陷导致其在需要闭环感知-推理循环的任务上大幅落后于人类。需要设计新的架构和训练目标来闭合感知-推理回路。

Abstract: Human vision is a closed loop: gaze is continuously redirected by intermediate hypotheses rather than a single snapshot. Decades of psychophysics and cognitive science have argued that this active observation is essential for a wide range of tasks. Whether today's multimodal large language models (MLLMs) exercise active observation is an empirical question that current vision-language benchmarks do not answer. We introduce ActiveVision, a benchmark that makes active observation measurable for MLLMs, comprising 17 tasks across 3 categories. Tasks are designed to force repeated visual perception rather than a single static description. Frontier MLLMs collapse on ActiveVision: the highest-scoring model we evaluate, GPT-5.5 at the highest exposed reasoning-effort tier, solves only 10.6% of items and scores zero on 11 of the 17 tasks, and even Claude Fable 5, despite topping most reasoning and coding leaderboards, solves just 3.5%, far behind three human participants who average 96.1%. Furthermore, much of the gap persists even when models write and run their own vision code: such code is unreliable on realistic imagery, and catching its failures itself requires the active perception the models lack. Together, these results indicate that current MLLMs lack robust active visual observation, motivating architectures and training objectives that close the perception-reasoning loop.

</details>


### [30] [Model Merging for Medical LVLMs: A Benchmark and a Winner-Take-All Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15661)
*Lichao Mou,Shilan Zhang,Chunlei Li,Bingcong Yan,Jingliang Hu,Yilei Shi,Shengwu Xiong,Xiao Xiang Zhu,Lei Li,Yaxiong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性研究医学大视觉语言模型(LVLM)的模型合并问题，提出包含8种影像模态、16个LoRA微调模型的MergeMedBench基准，并推出一种简单无超参数的"胜者全得"(winner-take-all)方法，通过保留专家模型中最主要的参数来避免信息稀释，在合并效果上一致优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学LVLM通过LoRA等参数高效微调方法可适配多种专业任务，但部署多个专家模型计算开销巨大；模型合并虽无需重训即可整合多专家，但该技术在医学领域尚未被系统探索。

Method: 构建MergeMedBench基准（8种影像模态、16个LoRA微调模型，覆盖两种主流架构）；提出"胜者全得"(winner-take-all)方法，即保留各专家LoRA中最关键的参数（权重绝对值最大的参数），丢弃较弱参数，避免平均或对齐策略带来的信息稀释问题。

Result: 在MergeMedBench上系统评估了现有合并方法，提出的winner-take-all方法尽管简单且无超参数，但一致优于所有现有方法。

Conclusion: Winner-take-all为LoRA模型合并提供了新视角，同时为未来研究建立了强有力的实践基线；表明在医学LVLM场景下，保留关键参数而非平均或对齐参数是更有效的合并策略。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) can be adapted to specialized medical imaging tasks via parameter-efficient fine-tuning approaches such as low-rank adaptation (LoRA), leading to a growing ecosystem of expert models tailored to specific imaging modalities and clinical scenarios. However, deploying multiple expert LVLMs in practice incurs substantial computational and operational overhead. Model merging provides a promising solution by consolidating multiple experts into a single model without retraining, yet it remains largely unexplored in the medical domain. In this work, we present the first systematic study of model merging for medical LVLMs. We introduce MergeMedBench, a comprehensive benchmark spanning eight imaging modalities and diverse clinical task types, comprising 16 LoRA fine-tuned models built upon two mainstream architectures. We conduct an extensive evaluation of existing merging methods and further propose winner-take-all, a simple and hyperparameter-free approach that retains only the most dominant parameters across expert models. By preserving the critical parameters that govern model behavior and discarding weaker ones, our method avoids the information dilution inherent in averaging- or alignment-based strategies. Despite its simplicity, winner-take-all consistently outperforms existing approaches, offering both a new perspective on LoRA merging and a strong practical baseline for future research.

</details>


### [31] [PE-Field 4D: Video Generation Models as Canvas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15667)
*Yunpeng Bai,Haoxiang Li,Qixing Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于位置编码的几何感知交叉注意力机制（PE-Field），通过在扩散Transformer中将参考内容的投影位置编码到目标视角，实现视频生成中视角变换和相机运动的可控性。该方法在相机重轨迹、新视角合成和几何感知视频编辑等任务上提升了空间可控性，同时保持原始视频扩散模型的生成先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散Transformer在视角变化和相机运动控制下难以精确控制场景几何，现有方法缺乏有效的几何感知引导机制。

Method: 提出几何感知交叉注意力机制：1) 将参考图像/帧的内容编码为结构化的上下文token；2) 对上下文token采用投影位置编码，结合目标视图2D重投影和深度感知消歧；3) 保留生成视频latent的原始时空位置编码，在注入几何引导的同时维持模型原有的latent结构一致性。

Result: 在相机重轨迹、新视角视频合成和几何感知视频编辑等视角依赖编辑任务中，实现了更好的空间可控性，同时保持了视频扩散模型的生成先验。

Conclusion: 重新审视了位置编码在视频扩散Transformer中的作用，证明其可为几何感知控制提供有效的空间偏置，提出的方法简洁有效，代码已开源。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers have recently achieved strong performance in video generation, yet controlling scene geometry under viewpoint changes and camera motion remains challenging. In this work, we revisit the role of positional encoding in video diffusion transformers and show that it provides a useful spatial bias for geometry-aware control. Specifically, if reference tokens are encoded according to their projected locations in the target view, the denoising model is encouraged to retrieve content from position aligned regions of the input video. Building on this observation, we introduce a geometry-aware cross-attention mechanism that enables target video latent tokens to attend to structured context tokens derived from reference images or frames. To establish correspondence between the reference content and the target camera trajectory, we equip the context tokens with a projected positional encoding scheme that combines target-view 2D reprojection with depth-aware disambiguation. At the same time, we preserve the original spatiotemporal positional encoding of the generated video latent, allowing geometric guidance to be injected while maintaining consistency with the video model's native latent structure. The resulting framework provides a simple and effective approach for controllable video generation. It improves spatial controllability in viewpoint-dependent editing tasks, including camera re-trajectory, novel-view video synthesis, and geometry-aware video editing, while preserving the generative prior of the underlying video diffusion model. The code is available at: https://github.com/MTLab/PE-Field.

</details>


### [32] [Efficient Frame Selection for Long Videos at Test Time with Attention-Based MLLM Selectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15689)
*Yilin Wang,Xiangxi Zheng,Dongxing Mao,Linjie Li,Zhengyuan Yang,Ping Yu,Rui Yan,Yuan Yao,Alex Jinpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DAFS，一种无需训练的动态注意力驱动的预算感知帧选择方法。利用MLLM中特定层交叉注意力产生的查询相关帧证据，通过轻量级2B参数选择器将注意力转化为相关性分数，并采用动态规划联合优化候选池大小和每帧token预算。在32帧预算下，相比均匀采样提升最多6.4个点，优于此前基于训练的选择器，且无需重训练即可泛化到不同骨干网络和任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解面临关键帧选择难题：选择关键帧需要先理解视频，但理解视频又依赖于选择了哪些帧，形成循环依赖。现有方法多采用均匀采样或训练复杂的选择器，效率与效果难以兼顾。

Method: 1. 发现MLLM中验证选择的交叉注意力层无需自回归生成即可提供查询相关帧证据；2. 构建DAFS：轻量级2B MLLM选择器，将选定层注意力通过查询条件聚合转化为相关性分数；3. 将候选池大小和每帧token预算的联合分配建模为离散优化问题，用动态规划求解。

Result: 32帧预算下，DAFS在Video-MME上比均匀采样提升最多6.4个点；优于此前基于训练的方法（匹配帧预算）；无需重训练即可泛化到不同选择器和回答器骨干网络及不同任务。

Conclusion: 跨模态注意力在特定层已蕴含查询相关的帧证据，可直接用于训练自由的帧选择；将选择与理解解耦，动态规划优化资源分配，可实现高效且泛化性强的长视频帧选择。

Abstract: Understanding long videos with multimodal large language models (MLLMs) requires selecting a compact set of frames from thousands of candidates, yet identifying the right frames seemingly requires understanding the video first. We resolve this circular dependency with a simple observation: cross-modal attention at validation-selected extraction layers in MLLMs already provides query-relevant frame evidence without requiring autoregressive generation. We exploit this property to build DAFS (Dynamic Attention-based Budget-aware Frame Selection), a training-free frame selector. A lightweight MLLM selector, even with only 2B parameters, can extract frame-level evidence by converting selected-layer attention into relevance scores through query-conditioned aggregation. This enables cross-frame comparison without autoregressive decoding. To handle the selector's own context constraint, we formulate the joint allocation of candidate pool size and per-frame token budget as a discrete optimization problem solved by dynamic programming. Under a 32-frame budget, our selector improves over uniform sampling by up to 6.4 points on Video-MME and outperforms prior training-based selectors under matched frame budgets, while generalizing across selector and answerer backbones, and across tasks, without retraining.

</details>


### [33] [Hierarchical Specialised Ensembles for Classification of Zebrafish Phenotypes Using the Selected Image Recognition Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15698)
*Piotr S. Maciąg,Monika Maciąg,Magdalena Majdan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了三种用于斑马鱼胚胎图像表型分类的分层集成方法，其中ConvNeXt骨干网络表现最优，专用化分层集成方案在F1分数上取得最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决斑马鱼胚胎表型分类问题，通过分层集成方法提升分类性能，探索不同骨干网络与集成策略的组合效果。

Method: 设计三种分层集成方案：阶段1使用四类分类器（Normal/Chorion/Dead/Other），阶段2对Other类分别采用单多标签分类器、两个专用多标签分类器或二元分类器集成；使用ResNet18、ViT和ConvNeXt三种骨干网络进行对比。

Result: ConvNeXt在所有方案中表现最佳；方案2的专用分层集成在F1分数上取得最佳平衡。

Conclusion: 专用分层集成方法对斑马鱼表型识别有效，ConvNeXt是推荐的骨干模型。

Abstract: We propose and evaluate three hierarchical ensemble setups for zebrafish phenotype classification from embryo images. In all setups, stage 1 uses a single four-class classifier to assign images to one of the exclusive phenotypes: Normal, Chorion, Dead, or Other. Images classified as Other are then processed in stage 2, where the ensemble design differs across setups: a single multi-label classifier, two specialized multi-label classifiers, or an ensemble of binary classifiers. We compare these setups using three backbone architectures: ResNet18, ViT, and ConvNeXt. Overall, ConvNeXt achieves the best performance across setups, while the specialized hierarchical ensemble in setup 2 provides the best balance in terms of F1-score. The results show that the proposed specialised hierarchical ensembles are effective for zebrafish phenotype recognition, and suggest that ConvNeXt is particularly useful backbone model.

</details>


### [34] [GoStop: Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Temporal Aggregation in Event-Based Feature Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15699)
*Youngho Kim,Hoonhee Cho,Jae-Young Kang,Kuk-Jin Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于强化学习的自适应事件累积框架，用于在线事件相机特征跟踪，通过训练RL智能体动态决定累积或推理时机，替代传统固定启发式规则，在动态运动场景下提升跟踪鲁棒性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件相机特征跟踪方法依赖人工设定的固定启发式规则进行事件累积，无法适应不同运动动态（如突变运动或缓慢运动），导致性能下降。

Method: 将事件累积建模为序列决策问题，引入强化学习框架训练自适应智能体，根据运动线索决定继续累积事件还是执行跟踪推理；同时提出DEFT数据集评估动态运动鲁棒性。

Result: 该方法集成到现有特征跟踪方法后，一致优于启发式基线方法，在动态运动下鲁棒性更强，且在跟踪精度与效率之间取得更优平衡。

Conclusion: 基于强化学习的自适应事件累积策略有效替代手工规则，提升了事件相机特征跟踪在复杂运动场景下的适应性和性能。

Abstract: Feature tracking plays a fundamental role in understanding scene motion and supports various downstream tasks. Event cameras, with their high temporal resolution and asynchronous sensing, enable low-latency and motion-robust perception, making them well-suited for feature tracking under fast and non-linear motion. However, existing event-based feature tracking methods rely on fixed heuristic rules based on hand-tuning for event accumulation. Such strategies fail to adapt to diverse motion dynamics, leading to degraded performance under abrupt motion changes or low-motion scenarios. In this paper, we model event accumulation as a sequential decision-making problem and introduce reinforcement learning (RL) framework to adaptively control the accumulation process for online event-based feature tracking. Our approach trains a RL agent that decides whether to continue accumulating events or to perform tracking inference based on motion cues. The proposed adaptive temporal agent enables dynamic adaptation to varying motion patterns without relying on hand-crafted rules. Furthermore, we introduce a Dynamic Event-based Tracking (DEFT) dataset with dynamic motion distributions to evaluate the robustness of the feature tracking. Extensive experiments demonstrate that integrating our plug-and-play framework to existing feature tracking methods consistently outperforms heuristic-based approaches, improving robustness under dynamic motion while offering a better balance between tracking accuracy and efficiency. Our project codes and datasets are available at https://github.com/kmax2001/GoSTOP

</details>


### [35] [Efficient Difficulty-Aware Dynamic Routing for Diffusion-Based Real-World Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15711)
*Xue Wu,Kang Zhao,Kafeng Wang,Jianfei Chen,Jingwei Xin,Nannan Wang,Xinbo Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种难度感知动态路由（DDR）策略，通过预测图像恢复难度并动态分配不同容量的网络，同时调整VAE下采样率以保留高频细节，从而提升真实图像超分辨率的效率与效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的Real-ISR方法存在两个关键局限：(1) 对所有输入样本采用统一的处理范式，忽略了不同图像恢复难度的差异；(2) SD模型中VAE的激进降采样（8倍）导致不可逆的细粒度细节丢失。因此需要一种能自适应处理不同难度样本且保留更多高频信息的方法。

Method: 提出DDR策略：首先设计难度估计器预测每张输入图像的恢复代价，据此自动将图像分配到合适容量的网络；然后通过调节SD骨干中VAE的空间降采样率，构建一组不同容量的Real-ISR网络，在困难样本上保留更多高频信息，在简单样本上保持高效。

Result: 大量实验表明，所提方法在效率和效果上均优于最新的SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 通过动态路由和VAE降采样率调节，克服了现有Real-ISR方法"一刀切"的处理范式，实现了精度与效率的自适应平衡。

Abstract: Diffusion-based methods have achieved impressive performance in real-world image super-resolution (Real-ISR) by leveraging large pre-trained stable diffusion (SD) models as powerful generative priors. However, these methods still face two key limitations. First, existing SD-based one-step and multi-step Real-ISR approaches adopt a unified processing paradigm for all input samples, ignoring the varying restoration difficulty across images. Second, the aggressive resolution reduction of the VAE in SD models (e.g., 8x downsampling) leads to irreversible loss of fine-scale details, which cannot be recovered by the subsequent diffusion process.
  To address these limitations, we propose a Difficulty-aware Dynamic Routing (DDR) strategy that overcomes the rigid, one-size-fits-all processing paradigm. Specifically, we first design a difficulty estimator to predict the restoration cost of each input image, enabling automatic assignment to a network of appropriate capacity. Then, we construct a set of Real-ISR networks with varying model capacities by modulating the spatial downsampling ratio of the VAE in the SD backbone, thereby preserving more high-frequency information for challenging cases while maintaining efficiency for simpler inputs.
  Extensive experiments have demonstrated the superior efficiency and effectiveness of the proposed model compared to recent state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [36] [Per-Stroke Temporal Control for Text-to-Motion via Action Units and Action-Detection Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15717)
*Euijun Jung,Youngki Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于动作单元（AU）的时序事件表示，通过轻量级门控适配器将冻结的文生动作模型注入AU条件信号，并在推理时利用无训练分类器梯度修正时序残差，显著提升对单个动作节拍（如左右交替出拳）的时序控制精度和动作质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生动作模型能正确生成提示词描述的动作类型，但无法可靠控制每个动作节拍的具体落地时间（如四次交替出拳很少能生成四个可分离的节拍），缺乏对单个动作轨迹、类别、时间窗口和冲击时间的精细控制能力。

Method: 1) 定义动作单元（AU）作为类型化的时序事件，包含身体轨迹、动作类别、时间窗口和冲击时间；2) 设计轻量级门控适配器，将冻结的文生动作骨干模型注入双流AU条件信号（每动作节拍token + 每帧相位通道）；3) 推理时利用冻结的帧级检测器的无训练分类器梯度来修正时序残差。

Result: 在StrokeBench基准上，AU方法在单动作节拍的正确放置率上显著优于最强先验接口，同时在动作质量上达到文本级、时间区间级和帧级基线中的最佳水平，且提示的核心帧可作为额外的可控轴。

Conclusion: 动作单元（AU）能够有效提升文生动作模型对精细时序事件的控制能力，兼顾动作质量和时序精度，核心帧提示可作为新的控制维度。
